<script>
    var loginLink   = document.getElementById('login-link');
    var redirectUrl = document.getElementById('redirect-url');

    if (loginLink !== null) {
        loginLink.onclick = function () {
            if (this.href !== location.href) {
                this.href = this.getAttribute('href') + '?redirect=' + decodeURIComponent(location.href);
            }
        };
    }

    if (redirectUrl !== null) {
        var redirectValue = decodeURIComponent(location.href.split('?redirect=')[1]);

        redirectUrl.value = redirectValue && redirectValue !== 'undefined' ? redirectValue : null;
    }
</script>

    if (searchOverlayForm) {
        searchOverlayForm.action = '/' + siteInfo.profession.abbr.toLowerCase() + '/search';

    // Global Search Overlay
    function clickOrTouchSearch (event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        var overlayElm          = document.getElementById("search-overlay-wrapper");
        var overlaySearchField  = document.getElementById("search-overlay-text");
        var overlayDisplayStyle = overlayElm.style.display;

        if (!overlayDisplayStyle || overlayDisplayStyle === "none") {
            overlayElm.style.display = "block";
            overlaySearchField.focus();
        }
        else {
            overlayElm.style.display = "none";
        }

        document.getElementById('search-overlay-close-btn').addEventListener("click", function () {
            overlayElm.style.display = "none";
            overlaySearchField.value = "";
        });

        searchOverlayForm.addEventListener("submit", function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            if (overlaySearchField.value) {
                searchOverlayForm.submit();
            }
        });
    }

    function addToCartEvent (e) {
        var data = e.target;

        llFbpushAddToCart({
            content_ids: [ data.getAttribute('data-id') ],
            content_name: data.getAttribute('data-name'),
            content_type: 'product',
            value: data.getAttribute('data-price'),
            currency: 'USD',
        })
    }

    function llFbPushTimeOnPage() {
        setTimeout(function () {
            llFbPushCustomEvent('Time on Page 10 Sec');
        }, 10000)
    }

    function llFbPushViewContent (product, custom) {
        if (custom) {
            llFbPushEvent('ViewContent', custom);

            return;
        }

        var params = {
            content_ids: [ product.id ],
            content_type: 'product',
            content_name: product.name
        };

        llFbPushEvent('ViewContent', params);
    }

    function llFbpushAddToCart (params) {
        llFbPushEvent('AddToCart', params);
    }

    function llFbpushInitiateCheckout (params) {
        llFbPushEvent('InitiateCheckout', params);
    }

    function llFbpushPurchase (params) {
        llFbPushEvent('Purchase', params);
    }

    function llFbpushStartTrial () {
        llFbPushEvent('StartTrial', {
            value: '0.00',
            currency: 'USD',
            predicted_ltv: '0.00'
        });
    }

    function llFbPushEvent(eventName, params) {
        if (llNoFbScript()) {
            return;
        }

        fbq('track', eventName, params);
    }

    function llNoFbScript() {
        return typeof fbq === 'undefined';
    }

    function llFbPushCustomEvent(eventName, params) {
        if (llNoFbScript()) {
            return;
        }

        fbq('trackCustom', eventName, params);
    }
</script>
<script>
    var isMobile = {
        Android: function() {
            return navigator.userAgent.match(/Android/i);
        },
        BlackBerry: function() {
            return navigator.userAgent.match(/BlackBerry/i);
        },
        iOS: function() {
            return navigator.userAgent.match(/iPhone|iPad|iPod/i);
        },
        Opera: function() {
            return navigator.userAgent.match(/Opera Mini/i);
        },
        Windows: function() {
            return navigator.userAgent.match(/IEMobile/i) || navigator.userAgent.match(/WPDesktop/i);
        },
        any: function() {
            return (isMobile.Android() || isMobile.BlackBerry() || isMobile.iOS() || isMobile.Opera() || isMobile.Windows());
        }
    };

    // Make Sticky Nav to work nicely with banner
    $(window).scroll(function (event) {
        if (isMobile.any()) {
            return;

        }

        var y       = $(this).scrollTop();
        var $header = $('#header');

        if (!$header.hasClass('no-sticky')) {

            if (y > 20) {
                $header.addClass('fixed');
            } else {
                $header.removeClass('fixed');
            }

        }
    });
</script>
